BEGIN;



-- Bersihkan data migrasi April yang sama supaya aman dijalankan ulang
DELETE FROM "DebtMutation" WHERE description LIKE '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026]%';
DELETE FROM "CashTransaction" WHERE description LIKE '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026]%';

-- VALIDASI MASTER DATA WAJIB ADA
DO $$
DECLARE missing text;
BEGIN
  SELECT string_agg(v.name, ', ') INTO missing FROM (VALUES ('BCA'), ('BRI'), ('BRI CV'), ('CASH'), ('DANA'), ('JENIUS'), ('MANDIRI'), ('MANDIRI CV'), ('OVO'), ('SHOPEE')) AS v(name) WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "BankAccount" b WHERE b.name = v.name);
  IF missing IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'BankAccount belum ada: %', missing; END IF;
  SELECT string_agg(v.name, ', ') INTO missing FROM (VALUES ('BEBAN BONUS KARYAWAN'), ('BEBAN GAPOK'), ('BEBAN LAIN'), ('DEVIDEN (SHARE PROFIT)'), ('HPP'), ('IKLAN'), ('PENARIKAN MODAL'), ('PENDAPATAN CUST TF'), ('PENDAPATAN LAIN'), ('PENDAPATAN LAZADA'), ('PENDAPATAN PENCAIRAN BAC'), ('PENDAPATAN PENCAIRAN EVERPRO'), ('PENDAPATAN PENCAIRAN KA'), ('PENDAPATAN SHOPEE'), ('PENDAPATAN TIK TOK SHOP'), ('PENGELUARAN PIUTANG')) AS v(name) WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "Category" c WHERE c.name = v.name);
  IF missing IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Category belum ada: %', missing; END IF;
  SELECT string_agg(v.name, ', ') INTO missing FROM (VALUES ('ASURANSI DAN BPJS'), ('ATK'), ('BIAYA ADMIN'), ('BIAYA AIR'), ('BIAYA KOMUNIKASI'), ('BIAYA LAINYA'), ('BIAYA LISTRIK'), ('BIAYA PACKING'), ('BIAYA SEWA'), ('BIAYA WIFI'), ('BONUS BOLA TERAPI'), ('BONUS EARPLUG'), ('BUKU'), ('DANA DARURAT'), ('HOSTINGER'), ('INVENTARIS KANTOR'), ('ITEM BONUS ALAT PIJAT'), ('ITEM BONUS LAINNYA'), ('KEGIATAN SABTU'), ('LANGGANAN SOFTWARE'), ('MATERAI'), ('ONGKIR PAKET'), ('PAJAK'), ('PERJALANAN DINAS'), ('PROTEKSI COD'), ('REFUND DANA'), ('SEMINAR'), ('THR'), ('ZAKAT')) AS v(name) WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "SubCategory" sc JOIN "Category" c ON c.id = sc."categoryId" WHERE sc.name = v.name AND c.name = 'BEBAN LAIN');
  IF missing IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SubCategory BEBAN LAIN belum ada: %', missing; END IF;
END $$;

-- Subkategori yang dipakai: ASURANSI DAN BPJS, ATK, BIAYA ADMIN, BIAYA AIR, BIAYA KOMUNIKASI, BIAYA LAINYA, BIAYA LISTRIK, BIAYA PACKING, BIAYA SEWA, BIAYA WIFI, BONUS BOLA TERAPI, BONUS EARPLUG, BUKU, DANA DARURAT, HOSTINGER, INVENTARIS KANTOR, ITEM BONUS ALAT PIJAT, ITEM BONUS LAINNYA, KEGIATAN SABTU, LANGGANAN SOFTWARE, MATERAI, ONGKIR PAKET, PAJAK, PERJALANAN DINAS, PROTEKSI COD, REFUND DANA, SEMINAR, THR, ZAKAT

WITH ct AS (
  INSERT INTO "CashTransaction" (id, date, description, "bankAccountId", "categoryId", "subCategoryId", type, amount, "proofUrl", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
  VALUES (gen_random_uuid()::text, '2026-04-07T00:00:00.000+07:00', '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026][ROW:110] BRINIDJA/FITRIYANTO', (SELECT id FROM "BankAccount" WHERE name = 'MANDIRI' LIMIT 1), (SELECT id FROM "Category" WHERE name = 'PENGELUARAN PIUTANG' LIMIT 1), NULL, 'EXPENSE'::"TransactionType", 1000000, NULL, NOW(), NOW())
  RETURNING id
), existing_entity AS (
  SELECT id FROM "DebtEntity" WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER('MAS FITRIYANTO') AND type = 'PIUTANG'::"DebtType" LIMIT 1
), inserted_entity AS (
  INSERT INTO "DebtEntity" (id, name, type, "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
  SELECT gen_random_uuid()::text, 'MAS FITRIYANTO', 'PIUTANG'::"DebtType", false, NOW(), NOW()
  WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM existing_entity)
  RETURNING id
), entity_row AS (
  SELECT id FROM existing_entity UNION ALL SELECT id FROM inserted_entity LIMIT 1
)
INSERT INTO "DebtMutation" (id, "entityId", date, amount, type, description, "cashTransactionId", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
SELECT gen_random_uuid()::text, entity_row.id, '2026-04-07T00:00:00.000+07:00', 1000000, 'ADD_DEBT'::"DebtMutationType", '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026][ROW:110] Piutang bertambah - MAS FITRIYANTO - BRINIDJA/FITRIYANTO', ct.id, NOW(), NOW() FROM entity_row CROSS JOIN ct;

WITH ct AS (
  INSERT INTO "CashTransaction" (id, date, description, "bankAccountId", "categoryId", "subCategoryId", type, amount, "proofUrl", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
  VALUES (gen_random_uuid()::text, '2026-04-28T00:00:00.000+07:00', '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026][ROW:825] MAS MIRZA PINJAM BELI STROLER', (SELECT id FROM "BankAccount" WHERE name = 'JENIUS' LIMIT 1), (SELECT id FROM "Category" WHERE name = 'PENGELUARAN PIUTANG' LIMIT 1), NULL, 'EXPENSE'::"TransactionType", 2100000, NULL, NOW(), NOW())
  RETURNING id
), existing_entity AS (
  SELECT id FROM "DebtEntity" WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER('MAS MIRZA') AND type = 'PIUTANG'::"DebtType" LIMIT 1
), inserted_entity AS (
  INSERT INTO "DebtEntity" (id, name, type, "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
  SELECT gen_random_uuid()::text, 'MAS MIRZA', 'PIUTANG'::"DebtType", false, NOW(), NOW()
  WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM existing_entity)
  RETURNING id
), entity_row AS (
  SELECT id FROM existing_entity UNION ALL SELECT id FROM inserted_entity LIMIT 1
)
INSERT INTO "DebtMutation" (id, "entityId", date, amount, type, description, "cashTransactionId", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
SELECT gen_random_uuid()::text, entity_row.id, '2026-04-28T00:00:00.000+07:00', 2100000, 'ADD_DEBT'::"DebtMutationType", '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026][ROW:825] Piutang bertambah - MAS MIRZA - MAS MIRZA PINJAM BELI STROLER', ct.id, NOW(), NOW() FROM entity_row CROSS JOIN ct;

SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_debt_rows, SUM(amount) AS total_debt_amount FROM "DebtMutation" WHERE description LIKE '[MIGRASI CASH APR 2026]%';
